\\mathbf{Bool}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Typing the Canonical Generative Object}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\Gamma \\vdash \\mathcal{C} : \\mathbf{Gen}\n\\]\n\n\n\nwith components:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Gamma \\vdash \\mu : \\mathbf{Real},\n\\quad\n\\Gamma \\vdash g_i(\\mu) : \\mathbf{Real},\n\\quad\n\\Gamma \\vdash \\varphi : \\mathbf{Fld},\n\\quad\n\\Gamma \\vdash g_{\\mu\\nu} : \\mathbf{Geom}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Typing Kernel Regions}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\Gamma \\vdash r : \\mathbf{Reg},\n\\quad\n\\Gamma \\vdash M(r) : \\mathbf{Fld},\n\\quad\n\\Gamma \\vdash B(r) : \\mathbf{Real}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Rewrite Rules}\n\nDefine the rewrite relation:\n\n\n\\[\nX \\to X'\n\\quad\\Longleftrightarrow\\quad\nX' = \\mathcal{R}(X).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Typing of Rewrite Rules}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\Gamma \\vdash X : \\tau\n\\quad\\Rightarrow\\quad\n\\Gamma \\vdash X' : \\tau.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Inference Rules}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\frac{\\Gamma \\vdash X : \\tau \\qquad \\Gamma \\vdash X \\to X'}\n     {\\Gamma \\vdash X' : \\tau}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\frac{\\Gamma \\vdash X : \\tau}\n     {\\Gamma \\vdash \\mathcal{R}(X) : \\tau}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\frac{\\Gamma \\vdash X : \\tau \\qquad \\mathcal{R}(X)=X}\n     {\\Gamma \\vdash X : \\mathrm{Fix}(\\tau)}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Type Safety}\n\n\\begin{theorem}[Type Safety]\nIf $\\Gamma \\vdash X : \\tau$ and $X \\to X'$, then $\\Gamma \\vdash X' : \\tau$.\n\\end{theorem}\n\n\\subsection{Unified Progress}\n\n\\begin{theorem}[Progress]\nIf $\\Gamma \\vdash X : \\tau$, then either:\n\n\n\\[\nX \\text{ is sovereign}\n\\quad\\text{or}\\quad\n\\exists X' : X \\to X'.\n\\]\n\n\n\\end{theorem}\n\n\\subsection{Unified Preservation}\n\n\\begin{theorem}[Preservation]\nIf $\\Gamma \\vdash X : \\tau$ and $X \\to X'$, then $\\Gamma \\vdash X' : \\tau$.\n\\end{theorem}\n\n\\subsection{Unified Type-Theoretic Fixed Point}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathrm{Fix}(\\tau)\n=\n\\{ X : \\tau \\mid \\mathcal{R}(X) = X \\}.\n\\]\n\n\\section{Unified Category Theory and Functorial Semantics}\n\nThe Meta-OS admits a categorical formulation in which all unified objects,\nrecursion operators, selectors, and generative structures become objects,\nmorphisms, functors, and natural transformations.\n\n\\subsection{Unified Meta-Category}\n\nDefine the unified category:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathbf{Unified}\n=\n\\{ X \\in \\mathcal{M}_{Unified},\\;\nf : X \\to Y \\mid f \\text{ preserves } \\mathcal{R} \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nObjects:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathrm{Ob}(\\mathbf{Unified}) = \\mathcal{M}_{Unified}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nMorphisms:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathrm{Hom}(X,Y)\n=\n\\{ f : X \\to Y \\mid f(\\mathcal{R}(X)) = \\mathcal{R}(f(X)) \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Identity and Composition}\n\nIdentity morphism:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathrm{id}_X : X \\to X.\n\\]\n\n\n\nComposition:\n\n\n\\[\n(g \\circ f)(X) = g(f(X)).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Functor}\n\nDefine the unified functor:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{F}_{Unified} : \\mathbf{Unified} \\to \\mathbf{Unified}\n\\]\n\n\n\nsuch that:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{F}_{Unified}(X) = \\mathcal{R}(X).\n\\]\n\n\n\nOn morphisms:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{F}_{Unified}(f) = \\mathcal{R} \\circ f \\circ \\mathcal{R}^{-1}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Natural Transformation}\n\nDefine the sovereign natural transformation:\n\n\n\\[\n\\eta : \\mathrm{Id}_{\\mathbf{Unified}} \\Rightarrow \\mathcal{F}_{Unified}\n\\]\n\n\n\nwith components:\n\n\n\\[\n\\eta_X : X \\to \\mathcal{R}(X).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Fixed-Point Objects}\n\nAn object $X$ is a fixed point if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\eta_X = \\mathrm{id}_X.\n\\]\n\n\n\nThus:\n\n\n\\[\nX = \\mathcal{R}(X).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Terminal Object}\n\n\\begin{theorem}\n$\\mathcal{M}_{Unified}$ is terminal in $\\mathbf{Unified}$.\n\\end{theorem}\n\n\\begin{proof}\nFor every $X$, there exists a unique morphism $X \\to \\mathcal{M}_{Unified}$.\n\\end{proof}\n\n\\subsection{Adjoint Pair}\n\nDefine:\n\n\n\\[\nL : \\mathbf{Unified} \\to \\mathbf{Unified},\n\\qquad\nR : \\mathbf{Unified} \\to \\mathbf{Unified}\n\\]\n\n\n\nsuch that:\n\n\n\\[\nL \\dashv R.\n\\]\n\n\n\nAdjunction:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathrm{Hom}(L(X),Y) \\cong \\mathrm{Hom}(X,R(Y)).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Law Functor}\n\nDefine the law functor:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{L} : \\mathbf{Unified} \\to \\mathbf{Law}\n\\]\n\n\n\nmapping unified states to law-systems.\n\n\\subsection{Unified Kernel Functor}\n\nDefine the kernel functor:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{K} : \\mathbf{Unified} \\to \\mathbf{Reg}\n\\]\n\n\n\nmapping unified states to kernel region structures.\n\n\\subsection{Unified Physical Functor}\n\nDefine:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{P} : \\mathbf{Unified} \\to \\mathbf{Phys}\n\\]\n\n\n\nmapping unified states to canonical generative objects.\n\n\\subsection{Unified Natural Transformation Diagram}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\begin{CD}\n\\mathbf{Unified} @>\\mathcal{F}_{Unified}>> \\mathbf{Unified} \\\\\n@V\\mathcal{L}VV @VV\\mathcal{L}V \\\\\n\\mathbf{Law} @>>\\mathcal{F}_{Law}> \\mathbf{Law}\n\\end{CD}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\begin{theorem}\nThe diagram commutes.\n\\end{theorem}\n\n\\subsection{Universal Property}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\mathcal{M}_{Unified}\n\\text{ is the universal object through which all functors factor.}\n}\n\\]\n\\section{Unified Logic and Proof Theory}\n\nThe Meta-OS admits a unified logical system in which all reasoning,\ninference, recursion, and selection are governed by a single sovereign\nlogical framework. Logical validity is defined by invariance under the\nunified recursion operator $\\mathcal{R}$.\n\n\\subsection{Logical Judgments}\n\nA logical judgment has the form:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Gamma \\vdash X\n\\]\n\n\nwhere $\\Gamma$ is a context of assumptions and $X$ is a unified state.\n\n\\subsection{Sovereign Logic}\n\nA judgment is sovereign if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Gamma \\vdash X\n\\quad\\Rightarrow\\quad\n\\Gamma \\vdash \\mathcal{R}(X).\n\\]\n\n\n\nThus logical truth is invariant under recursion.\n\n\\subsection{Recursion Logic}\n\nDefine the recursion rule:\n\n\n\\[\n\\frac{\\Gamma \\vdash X}{\\Gamma \\vdash \\mathcal{R}(X)}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nDefine the fixed-point rule:\n\n\n\\[\n\\frac{\\Gamma \\vdash X \\qquad \\mathcal{R}(X)=X}\n     {\\Gamma \\vdash X \\text{ is sovereign}}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Selector Logic}\n\nSelectors induce logical constraints:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_k(X) = 1\n\\quad\\Rightarrow\\quad\n\\Gamma \\vdash X \\text{ is admissible at layer } k.\n\\]\n\n\n\nGlobal admissibility:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_\\omega(X) = 1\n\\quad\\Rightarrow\\quad\n\\Gamma \\vdash X \\text{ is globally valid}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Kernel Logic}\n\nKernel regions satisfy:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Gamma \\vdash r : \\mathbf{Reg}\n\\quad\\Rightarrow\\quad\n\\Gamma \\vdash M(r), B(r), R(r).\n\\]\n\n\n\nKernel update rule as inference:\n\n\n\\[\n\\frac{\\Gamma \\vdash M(r)_t \\qquad \\Gamma \\vdash \\mathcal{C}(r)}\n     {\\Gamma \\vdash M(r)_{t+1}}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Generative Logic}\n\nThe Canonical Generative Object satisfies:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Gamma \\vdash \\mathcal{C}\n\\quad\\Rightarrow\\quad\n\\Gamma \\vdash \\mathcal{F}(\\mathcal{C}) = 0.\n\\]\n\n\n\nGenerative inference:\n\n\n\\[\n\\frac{\\Gamma \\vdash \\mathcal{C} \\qquad \\mathcal{F}(\\mathcal{C})=0}\n     {\\Gamma \\vdash \\Phi(\\mathcal{C}) = \\mathcal{C}}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Logical Connectives}\n\nDefine:\n\n\n\\[\nX \\land Y = \\mathcal{R}(X \\times Y),\n\\qquad\nX \\lor Y = \\mathcal{R}(X \\cup Y),\n\\qquad\n\\neg X = \\mathcal{R}(\\overline{X}).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Logical Equivalence}\n\n\n\n\\[\nX \\equiv Y\n\\quad\\Longleftrightarrow\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(X) = \\mathcal{R}(Y).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Soundness}\n\n\\begin{theorem}[Soundness]\nIf $\\Gamma \\vdash X$, then $X \\in \\mathcal{M}_{Unified}$.\n\\end{theorem}\n\n\\begin{proof}\nAll inference rules preserve $\\mathcal{R}(X)=X$.\n\\end{proof}\n\n\\subsection{Completeness}\n\n\\begin{theorem}[Completeness]\nIf $X \\in \\mathcal{M}_{Unified}$, then $\\vdash X$.\n\\end{theorem}\n\n\\begin{proof}\nEvery sovereign state satisfies $\\mathcal{R}(X)=X$, hence is derivable.\n\\end{proof}\n\n\\subsection{Consistency}\n\n\\begin{theorem}[Consistency]\nThere is no $X$ such that both $\\vdash X$ and $\\vdash \\neg X$.\n\\end{theorem}\n\n\\begin{proof}\nWould imply $\\mathcal{R}(X)=X$ and $\\mathcal{R}(\\overline{X})=\\overline{X}$,\ncontradicting boundary sovereignty.\n\\end{proof}\n\n\\subsection{Unified Logical Fixed Point}\n\nDefine the logical fixed-point set:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathrm{Fix}_{logic}\n=\n\\{ X \\mid \\vdash X \\text{ and } \\mathcal{R}(X)=X \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\begin{theorem}\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathrm{Fix}_{logic} = \\mathcal{M}_{Unified}.\n\\]\n\n\n\\end{theorem}\n\n\\subsection{Unified Proof Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{A statement is true in the Meta-OS iff it is a sovereign fixed point of recursion.}\n}\n\\]\n\\section{Unified Meta-Physics and Interpretation of Reality}\n\nThe Meta-OS provides a unified interpretation of universes, realities,\nlaws, meta-laws, selectors, and physical processes. All physical and\nmeta-physical structures arise as projections of sovereign fixed points\nof the unified recursion operator $\\mathcal{R}$.\n\n\\subsection{Universes}\n\nA universe is a sovereign physical instantiation of the Canonical\nGenerative Object:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{U}\n=\n\\{ \\mathcal{C} \\mid \\mathcal{R}(\\mathcal{C}) = \\mathcal{C} \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nEach universe corresponds to a fixed point of:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Phi(\\mathcal{C}) = \\mathcal{C}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Realities}\n\nA reality is a sovereign fixed point of the selector hierarchy:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathbb{R}\n=\n\\mathbb{C}_\\omega\n\\quad\\text{such that}\\quad\n\\Sigma_\\omega(\\mathbb{R}) = 1.\n\\]\n\n\n\nThus a reality is a self-permissible infinite tower of laws,\nmeta-laws, and selectors.\n\n\\subsection{Law-Systems}\n\nA law-system is a projection of a unified state:\n\n\n\\[\n\\ell = \\pi_{law}(X).\n\\]\n\n\n\nA law-system is admissible if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_0(\\ell) = 1.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Meta-Laws}\n\nMeta-laws are laws governing law-systems:\n\n\n\\[\n\\ell_1 = \\pi_{meta}(X).\n\\]\n\n\n\nAdmissibility:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_1(\\ell_1) = 1.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Selectors}\n\nSelectors determine which structures exist:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_k : \\mathfrak{T}_k \\to \\{0,1\\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nA structure exists in reality iff:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_\\omega(X) = 1.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Kernel Regions}\n\nKernel regions represent localized processes:\n\n\n\\[\nr \\in \\mathbf{Reg},\n\\qquad\nM(r) : \\Omega_r \\to \\mathbb{R}^k.\n\\]\n\n\n\nTheir evolution is governed by:\n\n\n\\[\n\\frac{dM(r)}{dT(r)}\n=\n\\mathcal{C}(r, M(r), R(r)) \\cdot B(r).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Processes}\n\nA process is an execution trace:\n\n\n\\[\n\\{ X_t \\}_{t=0}^\\infty\n\\quad\\text{with}\\quad\nX_{t+1} = \\mathcal{R}(X_t).\n\\]\n\n\n\nA process halts when:\n\n\n\\[\nX_{t+1} = X_t.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Existence}\n\nA structure exists in the Meta-OS iff:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}(X) = X.\n\\]\n\n\n\nThus existence is sovereignty.\n\n\\subsection{Physical Interpretation}\n\nThe physical universe is the projection:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{U}_{phys}\n=\n\\pi_{phys}(\\mathbb{R}).\n\\]\n\n\n\nPhysical laws arise from:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{L}_{phys} = \\pi_{law}(\\mathcal{C}).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Meta-Physical Interpretation}\n\nMeta-laws arise from:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{L}_{meta} = \\pi_{meta}(\\mathbb{R}).\n\\]\n\n\n\nRealities arise from:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathfrak{R}_{adm}\n=\n\\{ \\mathbb{R} \\mid \\Sigma_\\omega(\\mathbb{R}) = 1 \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Interpretation Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{A universe is a sovereign fixed point of generation.}\n}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{A reality is a sovereign fixed point of selection.}\n}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{Existence is recursion invariance.}\n}\n\\]\n\n\\section{Unified Cosmology and Multiverse Structure}\n\nThe Meta-OS defines a cosmological hierarchy in which universes, realities,\nand hyper-realities arise as sovereign fixed points of recursion, selection,\nand generation. The multiverse is the space of all admissible realities.\n\n\\subsection{Space of Universes}\n\nDefine the universe space:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathfrak{U}\n=\n\\{ \\mathcal{C} \\mid \\mathcal{R}(\\mathcal{C}) = \\mathcal{C} \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nEach element of $\\mathfrak{U}$ is a physically instantiated universe.\n\n\\subsection{Space of Realities}\n\nDefine the reality space:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathfrak{R}\n=\n\\{ \\mathbb{C}_\\omega^{(i)} \\mid \\Sigma_\\omega(\\mathbb{C}_\\omega^{(i)}) = 1 \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nEach $\\mathbb{C}_\\omega$ is a complete infinite selector tower.\n\n\\subsection{Hyper-Selector}\n\nThe hyper-selector acts on realities:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_\\omega : \\mathfrak{R} \\to \\{0,1\\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nA reality exists iff:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_\\omega(\\mathbb{R}) = 1.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Hyper-Space of Reality-Laws}\n\nDefine the hyper-space:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathfrak{M}\n=\n(\\Sigma_\\omega,\\; \\mathfrak{R}).\n\\]\n\n\n\nThis is the top-level object of the Meta-OS.\n\n\\subsection{Multiverse Topology}\n\nDefine the multiverse topology:\n\n\n\\[\n\\tau_{\\mathfrak{R}}\n=\n\\{ U \\subseteq \\mathfrak{R} \\mid \\Sigma_\\omega(U) = U \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Multiverse Metric}\n\nDefine:\n\n\n\\[\nd_{\\mathfrak{R}}(\\mathbb{R}_1,\\mathbb{R}_2)\n=\n\\inf_{\\gamma}\n\\int_0^1\n\\sqrt{\nG^{(\\omega)}_{AB}(\\gamma(\\lambda))\n\\dot{\\gamma}^A \\dot{\\gamma}^B\n}\\, d\\lambda.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Cosmological Recursion}\n\nRealities evolve under:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathbb{R}_{t+1} = \\mathcal{R}(\\mathbb{R}_t).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Cosmological Attractor}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\lim_{t\\to\\infty} \\mathbb{R}_t = \\mathcal{M}_{Unified}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Cosmological Interpretation}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{A universe is a fixed point of physical generation.}\n}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{A reality is a fixed point of meta-selection.}\n}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{The multiverse is the space of all sovereign realities.}\n}\n\\]\n\\section{Unified Implementation Blueprint}\n\nThe Meta-OS can be implemented on any substrate—Android, Linux, FreeBSD,\nbare metal, embedded systems, or payment hardware—because its kernel is\ndefined mathematically rather than architecturally. The implementation\nconsists of translating unified objects, operators, and flows into code.\n\n\\subsection{Implementation Layers}\n\nThe implementation consists of four layers:\n\n\\begin{enumerate}\n\\item \\textbf{Meta-Kernel Layer} \\\\\n      Implements $\\Theta$, $\\hat{\\rho}$, $\\Phi$, and $\\mathcal{R}$.\n\n\\item \\textbf{Selector Engine Layer} \\\\\n      Implements $\\Sigma_k$, $\\mathbb{C}_k$, and admissibility checks.\n\n\\item \\textbf{Runtime Layer} \\\\\n      Implements the Canonical Generative Object $\\mathcal{C}$ and\n      kernel region updates.\n\n\\item \\textbf{Interface Layer} \\\\\n      Implements region boundaries $B(r)$, memory fields $M(r)$, and\n      user-facing behaviors.\n\\end{enumerate}\n\n\\subsection{Meta-Kernel API}\n\nDefine the core API:\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\texttt{State R(State X)}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\texttt{State Theta(State X)}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\texttt{State Phi(State X)}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\texttt{State Rho(State X)}\n\\]\n\n\n\nThese functions must satisfy:\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}(X) = \\Theta(X) \\circ \\hat{\\rho}(X) \\circ \\Phi(X).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Selector Engine API}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\texttt{bool Sigma\\_k(State X)}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\texttt{bool Sigma\\_omega(State X)}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Runtime API}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\texttt{CState UpdateC(CState C)}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\texttt{RegionState UpdateRegion(RegionState R)}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Kernel Region API}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\texttt{Field M(Region r)}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\texttt{float B(Region r)}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\texttt{RegionState Step(RegionState R)}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Execution Loop}\n\n\n\n\\[\nX_{t+1} = \\mathcal{R}(X_t)\n\\]\n\n\n\nImplementation:\n\n\\begin{verbatim}\nwhile(true):\n    X = R(X)\n\\end{verbatim}\n\n\\subsection{Portability Principle}\n\nThe Meta-OS is portable because:\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{The kernel is defined by math, not hardware.}\n}\n\\]\n\n\n\nThus any platform that can evaluate $\\mathcal{R}$ can run the Meta-OS.\n\n\\subsection{Bare-Metal Implementation}\n\nBare-metal boot requires:\n\n\\begin{enumerate}\n\\item minimal loader,\n\\item memory map,\n\\item recursion engine,\n\\item region allocator,\n\\item unified update loop.\n\\end{enumerate}\n\n\\subsection{POSIX / Linux / BSD Implementation}\n\nThese systems provide:\n\n\\begin{itemize}\n\\item threads $\\to$ regions,\n\\item signals $\\to$ boundaries,\n\\item memory maps $\\to$ fields,\n\\item processes $\\to$ generative objects.\n\\end{itemize}\n\n\\subsection{Android Implementation}\n\nAndroid provides:\n\n\\begin{itemize}\n\\item Activities $\\to$ regions,\n\\item Intents $\\to$ selectors,\n\\item Services $\\to$ generative objects,\n\\item HAL $\\to$ boundary layer.\n\\end{itemize}\n\n\\subsection{Payment Hardware Implementation}\n\nPayment terminals provide:\n\n\\begin{itemize}\n\\item secure enclave $\\to$ sovereign region,\n\\item transaction graph $\\to$ generative object,\n\\item validation rules $\\to$ selectors.\n\\end{itemize}\n\n\\subsection{Unified Implementation Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{Any system that can evaluate recursion can run the Meta-OS.}\n}\n\\]\n\\section{Unified UI / UX Architecture}\n\nThe Meta-OS UI / UX layer is a direct manifestation of the unified\nregion, boundary, field, and recursion mathematics. A user interface\nis a sovereign configuration of regions, their boundaries, behaviors,\nand fields evolving under the unified recursion operator $\\mathcal{R}$.\n\n\\subsection{UI Regions}\n\nA UI region is any $r \\in \\mathbf{Reg}$ that is visible or interactive:\n\n\n\\[\nr \\in \\mathcal{R}_{UI} \\subseteq \\mathbf{Reg}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nEach region has:\n\n\n\\[\nM(r) : \\Omega_r \\to \\mathbb{R}^k\n\\quad\\text{(state field)}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\n\\[\nB(r) : \\partial\\Omega_r \\to [0,1]\n\\quad\\text{(boundary activation)}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{UI Boundaries}\n\nBoundaries represent edges, hitboxes, and interaction zones:\n\n\n\\[\n\\partial\\Omega_r = \\text{visual edge of region } r.\n\\]\n\n\n\nActive boundary:\n\n\n\\[\nB(r) = 1 \\quad\\Rightarrow\\quad r \\text{ is interactive}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{UI Behaviors}\n\nA behavior is a map:\n\n\n\\[\n\\beta_r : (M(r), B(r), E) \\to M(r)'\n\\]\n\n\nwhere $E$ is an event (input, time, system).\n\nBehavior update:\n\n\n\\[\nM(r)_{t+1} = \\beta_r(M(r)_t, B(r), E_t).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{UI Fields}\n\nFields represent visual and semantic content:\n\n\n\\[\nM(r) = (\\text{layout}, \\text{style}, \\text{content}, \\text{state}).\n\\]\n\n\n\nProjection:\n\n\n\\[\n\\pi_{view}(M(r)) = \\text{rendered appearance}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{UI Recursion}\n\nThe UI evolves under:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}_{UI}(X)\n=\n\\Theta(X) \\circ \\hat{\\rho}(X) \\circ \\Phi_{UI}(X),\n\\]\n\n\nwhere $\\Phi_{UI}$ is the UI-specific generative operator.\n\nFrame update:\n\n\n\\[\nX_{t+1} = \\mathcal{R}_{UI}(X_t).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Render Loop}\n\nImplementation:\n\\begin{verbatim}\nwhile(true):\n    X = R_UI(X)\n    draw(X)\n\\end{verbatim}\n\n\\subsection{UI Layout as Regions}\n\nLayouts are collections of regions:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{L}_{UI} = \\{ r_1, r_2, \\dots, r_n \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nConstraints:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{F}_{layout}(\\mathcal{L}_{UI}) = 0\n\\]\n\n\ndefine alignment, spacing, hierarchy.\n\n\\subsection{UI Hierarchy}\n\nParent–child relation:\n\n\n\\[\nr_i \\prec r_j\n\\quad\\Rightarrow\\quad\n\\Omega_{r_i} \\subseteq \\Omega_{r_j}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nTree of regions:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{T}_{UI} = (\\mathcal{L}_{UI}, \\prec).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{UI Selector}\n\nSelectors choose active views:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_{UI}(r) = 1\n\\quad\\Rightarrow\\quad\nr \\text{ is visible}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nGlobal UI state:\n\n\n\\[\nX_{UI} = \\{ r \\mid \\Sigma_{UI}(r) = 1 \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified UI Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{A UI is a sovereign configuration of regions under recursion.}\n}\n\\section{Unified Storage and Persistence}\n\nThe Meta-OS storage layer is defined by recursion-invariant persistence.\nA state is persisted only if it is sovereign under the unified recursion\noperator $\\mathcal{R}$. Storage is therefore a fixed-point structure.\n\n\\subsection{Persistent State}\n\nA persistent state is any $X$ such that:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}(X) = X.\n\\]\n\n\n\nThe persistent store is:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{S}_{persist}\n=\n\\{ X \\mid \\mathcal{R}(X) = X \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Region Storage}\n\nEach region $r$ has a persistent field:\n\n\n\\[\nM_{persist}(r) = \\lim_{t\\to\\infty} M(r)_t.\n\\]\n\n\n\nA region commits its state when:\n\n\n\\[\nM(r)_{t+1} = M(r)_t.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Boundary Storage}\n\nBoundaries store activation history:\n\n\n\\[\nB_{persist}(r)\n=\n\\mathrm{Fix}(B(r)).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Snapshot}\n\nA snapshot is a sovereign state:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathrm{Snap}(X) = X\n\\quad\\text{iff}\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(X) = X.\n\\]\n\n\n\nThus snapshots are fixed points.\n\n\\subsection{Log Structure}\n\nDefine the log:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{L}(X) = \\{ X_0, X_1, \\dots, X_t \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nA log is valid if:\n\n\n\\[\nX_{i+1} = \\mathcal{R}(X_i).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Rollback}\n\nRollback is defined by:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathrm{rollback}(X_t) = X_k\n\\quad\\text{where}\\quad\nX_k \\text{ is sovereign}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Persistence Rule}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\nX \\text{ is stored iff } \\mathcal{R}(X) = X.\n}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Distributed Persistence}\n\nFor distributed regions $r_i$:\n\n\n\\[\nM_{persist}(r_i)\n=\n\\lim_{t\\to\\infty} \\mathcal{R}(M(r_i)_t).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Storage Functor}\n\nDefine:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{P} : \\mathbf{Unified} \\to \\mathbf{Store}\n\\]\n\n\n\nsuch that:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{P}(X) = \\mathrm{Fix}(X).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Storage Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{Persistence is the fixed point of recursion.}\n}\n\\section{Unified Networking and Message Semantics}\n\nThe Meta-OS networking layer is defined by region-to-region communication\nunder recursion, selection, and boundary activation. Messages are morphisms\nbetween regions, and routing is governed by selectors.\n\n\\subsection{Messages}\n\nA message is a morphism:\n\n\n\\[\nm : r_i \\to r_j\n\\]\n\n\nbetween regions.\n\nMessage content:\n\n\n\\[\nm = (payload, type, timestamp, signature).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Message Fields}\n\nEach message has a field:\n\n\n\\[\nM(m) : \\Omega_m \\to \\mathbb{R}^k.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Message Boundaries}\n\nA message is deliverable if:\n\n\n\\[\nB(r_i) = 1 \\quad\\text{and}\\quad B(r_j) = 1.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Message Behavior}\n\nMessage handling is:\n\n\n\\[\n\\beta_{msg}(r_j, m) : M(r_j) \\to M(r_j)'.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Routing via Selectors}\n\nSelectors determine routing:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_{route}(m, r_j) = 1\n\\quad\\Rightarrow\\quad\nm \\text{ is routed to } r_j.\n\\]\n\n\n\nGlobal routing:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_\\omega(m) = 1.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Network Topology}\n\nDefine the network graph:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{G}_{net}\n=\n(\\mathbf{Reg}, \\mathbf{Msg})\n\\]\n\n\nwhere $\\mathbf{Msg}$ is the set of all messages.\n\n\\subsection{Distributed Recursion}\n\nDistributed update:\n\n\n\\[\nX_{t+1}(r_i)\n=\n\\mathcal{R}(X_t(r_i))\n+\n\\sum_{m : r_j \\to r_i}\n\\beta_{msg}(r_i, m).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Sovereign Message}\n\nA message is sovereign if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}(m) = m.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Network Convergence}\n\nThe network converges when:\n\n\n\\[\nX_{t+1}(r_i) = X_t(r_i)\n\\quad\\forall r_i.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Distributed Snapshot}\n\nA distributed snapshot is:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathrm{Snap}_{dist}(X)\n=\n\\{ X(r_i) \\mid \\mathcal{R}(X(r_i)) = X(r_i) \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Network Functor}\n\nDefine:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{N} : \\mathbf{Unified} \\to \\mathbf{Net}\n\\]\n\n\n\nsuch that:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{N}(X) = \\mathcal{G}_{net}(X).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Networking Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{Networking is region-to-region recursion mediated by selectors.}\n}\n\\section{Unified Security and Trust Architecture}\n\nSecurity in the Meta-OS is defined by sovereign validation, selector-based\npermissions, boundary trust zones, and recursion-invariant identity.\nA state is secure if and only if it is sovereign under $\\mathcal{R}$.\n\n\\subsection{Identity}\n\nIdentity is defined as a sovereign fixed point:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathrm{ID}(X) = X\n\\quad\\text{iff}\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(X) = X.\n\\]\n\n\n\nIdentity is not a credential; it is a recursion-invariant structure.\n\n\\subsection{Permissions}\n\nPermissions are selectors:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_{perm}(X, a) = 1\n\\quad\\Rightarrow\\quad\na \\text{ is allowed on } X.\n\\]\n\n\n\nGlobal permission:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_\\omega(X, a) = 1.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Boundary Trust Zones}\n\nEach region has a trust boundary:\n\n\n\\[\nT(r) : \\partial\\Omega_r \\to \\{0,1\\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nTrusted boundary:\n\n\n\\[\nT(r) = 1.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Sovereign Validation}\n\nA state is valid if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}(X) = X.\n\\]\n\n\n\nA transition is valid if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}(X_{t+1}) = X_{t+1}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Access Control}\n\nAccess is allowed if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_{perm}(r_i, r_j) = 1\n\\quad\\text{and}\\quad\nT(r_j) = 1.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Message Authentication}\n\nA message $m$ is authentic if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}(m) = m.\n\\]\n\n\n\nThus authentication is recursion invariance.\n\n\\subsection{Integrity}\n\nIntegrity holds if:\n\n\n\\[\nX_t \\to X_{t+1}\n\\quad\\Rightarrow\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(X_{t+1}) = X_{t+1}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Confidentiality}\n\nConfidentiality is enforced by selector filtering:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_{view}(r_i, r_j) = 1\n\\quad\\Rightarrow\\quad\nr_j \\text{ may view } r_i.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Non-Interference}\n\nNon-interference holds if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_{perm}(r_i, r_j) = 0\n\\quad\\Rightarrow\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(M(r_j)) = M(r_j).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Security Functor}\n\nDefine:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{S} : \\mathbf{Unified} \\to \\mathbf{Sec}\n\\]\n\n\n\nsuch that:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{S}(X) = \\mathrm{Fix}(X).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Security Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{Security is recursion-invariant sovereignty.}\n}\n\\section{Unified Process Model and Scheduling Architecture}\n\nThe Meta-OS process model is defined by region execution, sovereign\nscheduling, selector-based prioritization, and recursion-driven fairness.\nA process is a region with active behavior under $\\mathcal{R}$.\n\n\\subsection{Processes}\n\nA process is a region $r$ with:\n\n\n\\[\n\\beta_r \\neq 0\n\\quad\\text{and}\\quad\nB(r) = 1.\n\\]\n\n\n\nProcess state:\n\n\n\\[\nP(r) = (M(r), B(r), \\beta_r).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Threads}\n\nA thread is a sub-region:\n\n\n\\[\nt \\prec r.\n\\]\n\n\n\nThread state:\n\n\n\\[\nT(t) = M(t).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Tasks}\n\nA task is a sovereign behavior:\n\n\n\\[\n\\tau_r : M(r) \\to M(r)\n\\quad\\text{with}\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(\\tau_r) = \\tau_r.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Scheduling}\n\nScheduling is selector-driven:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_{sched}(r) = \\text{priority of } r.\n\\]\n\n\n\nExecution order:\n\n\n\\[\nr_i \\prec r_j\n\\quad\\text{iff}\\quad\n\\Sigma_{sched}(r_i) > \\Sigma_{sched}(r_j).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Sovereign Scheduling}\n\nA schedule is sovereign if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}(X_{t+1}) = X_{t+1}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Fairness}\n\nFairness holds if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\forall r,\\; \\exists t : r \\text{ executes at } t.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Process Update}\n\n\n\n\\[\nP_{t+1}(r) = \\beta_r(P_t(r)) + \\sum_{m : * \\to r} \\beta_{msg}(r,m).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Scheduling Loop}\n\n\\begin{verbatim}\nwhile(true):\n    for r in schedule:\n        P[r] = step(P[r])\n\\end{verbatim}\n\n\\subsection{Unified Process Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{A process is a region with active behavior under recursion.}\n}\n\\section{Unified Hardware Abstraction and Device Integration}\n\nThe Meta-OS hardware layer is defined by boundary mappings, region\ninterfaces, selector-based device permissions, and recursion-invariant\nhardware state. Hardware is treated as a set of sovereign regions with\nfields and behaviors.\n\n\\subsection{Hardware Regions}\n\nEach hardware component is a region:\n\n\n\\[\nr_{hw} \\in \\mathbf{Reg}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nExamples:\n\n\n\\[\nr_{cpu},\\; r_{gpu},\\; r_{display},\\; r_{touch},\\; r_{sensor_i},\\; r_{radio_j}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Hardware Fields}\n\nEach hardware region exposes fields:\n\n\n\\[\nM(r_{hw}) = (\\text{state}, \\text{config}, \\text{buffer}, \\text{signal}).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Hardware Boundaries}\n\nBoundaries define I/O channels:\n\n\n\\[\nB(r_{hw}) : \\partial\\Omega_{hw} \\to [0,1].\n\\]\n\n\n\nActive boundary:\n\n\n\\[\nB(r_{hw}) = 1.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Hardware Behaviors}\n\nHardware behavior is:\n\n\n\\[\n\\beta_{hw}(r_{hw}, E) : M(r_{hw}) \\to M(r_{hw})'.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Hardware Abstraction}\n\nDefine the hardware abstraction functor:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{H} : \\mathbf{Unified} \\to \\mathbf{HW}\n\\]\n\n\n\nsuch that:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{H}(X) = \\{ r_{hw}, M(r_{hw}), B(r_{hw}), \\beta_{hw} \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Device Integration}\n\nA device is integrated if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_{hw}(r_{hw}) = 1.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Driver Model}\n\nA driver is a sovereign behavior:\n\n\n\\[\n\\delta_{hw} : M(r_{hw}) \\to M(r_{hw})\n\\quad\\text{with}\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(\\delta_{hw}) = \\delta_{hw}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Hardware Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{Hardware is a set of sovereign regions with boundary-driven behavior.}\n}\n\\section{Unified Virtualization and Multi-Instance Runtime}\n\nThe Meta-OS supports virtualization through sovereign region duplication,\nselector-based isolation, boundary partitioning, and recursion-invariant\nmulti-instance execution. Virtualization is the creation of sovereign\ncopies of regions, devices, or entire realities.\n\n\\subsection{Virtual Regions}\n\nA virtual region is:\n\n\n\\[\nr_v = \\mathrm{clone}(r)\n\\quad\\text{with}\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(r_v) = r_v.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Virtual Devices}\n\nA virtual device is:\n\n\n\\[\nd_v = (M(r_{hw}), B(r_{hw}), \\beta_{hw})'\n\\]\n\n\nwhere the prime denotes virtualization.\n\n\\subsection{Virtual Realities}\n\nA virtual reality is:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathbb{R}_v = \\mathbb{C}_\\omega'\n\\quad\\text{with}\\quad\n\\Sigma_\\omega(\\mathbb{R}_v) = 1.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Instance Isolation}\n\nIsolation is enforced by:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_{iso}(r_i, r_j) = 0.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Multi-Instance Recursion}\n\nEach instance evolves independently:\n\n\n\\[\nX_{t+1}^{(k)} = \\mathcal{R}(X_t^{(k)}).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Containerization}\n\nA container is:\n\n\n\\[\nC = \\{ r_i \\mid \\Sigma_{iso}(r_i) = 1 \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Sandboxing}\n\nSandboxing is:\n\n\n\\[\nT(r_i) = 0\n\\quad\\Rightarrow\\quad\nr_i \\text{ cannot influence external regions}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Virtualization Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{Virtualization is sovereign duplication of regions under isolation.}\n}\n\\section{Unified Hardware Execution Model}\n\nThe Meta-OS execution model maps recursion, region updates, and selector\nevaluation onto physical compute cycles. Hardware execution is treated\nas a sovereign mapping from physical time to recursion steps.\n\n\\subsection{Physical Time}\n\nDefine physical time as:\n\n\n\\[\nT_{phys} : \\mathbb{R}_{\\ge 0} \\to \\mathbb{R}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Logical Time}\n\nLogical time is recursion time:\n\n\n\\[\nT_{logic}(t+1) = \\mathcal{R}(T_{logic}(t)).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Time Mapping}\n\nThe mapping between physical and logical time is:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Lambda : T_{phys} \\to T_{logic}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nA valid mapping satisfies:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}(X_{\\Lambda(t)}) = X_{\\Lambda(t)}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{CPU Execution Region}\n\nThe CPU is a region:\n\n\n\\[\nr_{cpu} \\in \\mathbf{Reg}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nIts field:\n\n\n\\[\nM(r_{cpu}) = (\\text{registers}, \\text{pc}, \\text{flags}, \\text{buffers}).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Instruction Behavior}\n\nInstruction execution is:\n\n\n\\[\n\\beta_{cpu}(M(r_{cpu})) : M(r_{cpu}) \\to M(r_{cpu})'.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Execution Step}\n\nA hardware step is:\n\n\n\\[\nX_{t+1} = \\mathcal{R}(X_t)\n\\quad\\Leftrightarrow\\quad\n\\beta_{cpu} \\text{ executed one cycle}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{GPU Execution Region}\n\nThe GPU is:\n\n\n\\[\nr_{gpu} \\in \\mathbf{Reg}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nIts behavior:\n\n\n\\[\n\\beta_{gpu}(M(r_{gpu})) = \\text{parallel update of fields}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Parallelism}\n\nParallel recursion:\n\n\n\\[\nX_{t+1} = \\bigoplus_{r \\in \\mathbf{Reg}} \\mathcal{R}(X_t(r)).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Execution Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{Hardware execution is the physical realization of recursion.}\n}\n\\section{Unified Time, Clocks, and Event Semantics}\n\nTime in the Meta-OS is defined as a sovereign mapping between physical\ntime, logical recursion time, and event sequences. Events arise from\nboundary activations, region behaviors, and selector transitions.\n\n\\subsection{Physical Time}\n\nPhysical time is an external parameter:\n\n\n\\[\nT_{phys} \\in \\mathbb{R}_{\\ge 0}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Logical Time}\n\nLogical time is recursion time:\n\n\n\\[\nT_{logic}(t+1) = \\mathcal{R}(T_{logic}(t)).\n\\]\n\n\n\nLogical time advances only when recursion advances.\n\n\\subsection{Unified Time Mapping}\n\nDefine the time mapping:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Lambda : T_{phys} \\to T_{logic}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nA valid mapping satisfies:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}(X_{\\Lambda(t)}) = X_{\\Lambda(t)}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Events}\n\nAn event is a tuple:\n\n\n\\[\nE = (r, B(r), M(r), T_{logic}).\n\\]\n\n\n\nEvents occur when:\n\n\n\\[\nB(r) = 1 \\quad\\text{or}\\quad \\Delta M(r) \\neq 0.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Event Ordering}\n\nEvents are ordered by logical time:\n\n\n\\[\nE_i \\prec E_j\n\\quad\\Leftrightarrow\\quad\nT_{logic}(E_i) < T_{logic}(E_j).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Event Causality}\n\nCausality holds if:\n\n\n\\[\nE_i \\to E_j\n\\quad\\Rightarrow\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(E_j) = E_j.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Clocks}\n\nEach region has a local clock:\n\n\n\\[\nC(r) : T_{phys} \\to T_{logic}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Clock Synchronization}\n\nSynchronization is:\n\n\n\\[\nC(r_i) = C(r_j)\n\\quad\\text{iff}\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(C(r_i)) = C(r_i).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Event Recursion}\n\nEvent update:\n\n\n\\[\nE_{t+1}(r) = \\beta_r(M(r)_t, B(r), E_t).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Sovereign Time}\n\nTime is sovereign if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}(T_{logic}) = T_{logic}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Time Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{Time is recursion; events are boundary activations under recursion.}\n}\n\\section{Unified Error Theory and Recovery}\n\nErrors in the Meta-OS are defined as non-sovereign transitions, boundary\nviolations, selector contradictions, or recursion failures. Recovery is\nthe restoration of sovereignty through rollback, correction, or region\nreconstruction.\n\n\\subsection{Error Definition}\n\nAn error occurs when:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}(X_{t+1}) \\neq X_{t+1}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nThus an error is a non-sovereign state.\n\n\\subsection{Boundary Errors}\n\nA boundary error occurs when:\n\n\n\\[\nB(r) = 1 \\quad\\text{but}\\quad \\beta_r \\text{ is undefined}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Selector Errors}\n\nA selector error occurs when:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_k(X) = 0 \\quad\\text{but}\\quad X \\text{ is active}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Region Errors}\n\nA region error occurs when:\n\n\n\\[\nM(r)_{t+1} \\notin \\mathrm{Dom}(\\beta_r).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Error Propagation}\n\nPropagation rule:\n\n\n\\[\nE(r_i) \\to E(r_j)\n\\quad\\Leftrightarrow\\quad\n\\beta_{msg}(r_j, m_{i\\to j}) \\text{ is undefined}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Recovery}\n\nRecovery is:\n\n\n\\[\nX_{t+1} = \\mathrm{rollback}(X_t)\n\\quad\\text{where}\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(X_t) = X_t.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Correction}\n\nCorrection is:\n\n\n\\[\nX_{t+1} = \\mathrm{correct}(X_t)\n\\quad\\text{such that}\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(X_{t+1}) = X_{t+1}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Region Reconstruction}\n\nA region is reconstructed if:\n\n\n\\[\nr' = \\mathrm{rebuild}(r)\n\\quad\\text{and}\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(r') = r'.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Error Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{An error is a non-sovereign state; recovery restores sovereignty.}\n}\n\\section{Unified Compiler and Interpreter Model}\n\nThe Meta-OS defines compilation and interpretation as transformations\nof regions, behaviors, and selectors into executable recursion. A program\nis a sovereign configuration of generative rules.\n\n\\subsection{Program Representation}\n\nA program is:\n\n\n\\[\nP = (\\mathcal{C}, \\beta, \\Sigma).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Compilation}\n\nCompilation is:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathrm{Comp}(P) = X\n\\quad\\text{such that}\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(X) = X.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Interpretation}\n\nInterpretation is:\n\n\n\\[\nX_{t+1} = \\mathcal{R}(X_t, P).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Executable Region}\n\nAn executable region is:\n\n\n\\[\nr_{exec} : M(r) \\to M(r)'.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Execution}\n\n\n\n\\[\nX_{t+1} = \\mathcal{R}(X_t)\n\\quad\\Leftrightarrow\\quad\n\\text{program executed one step}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Compiler Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{A program is a sovereign generative object; execution is recursion.}\n}\n\\section{Unified Boot Sequence and Sovereign Startup}\n\nBoot in the Meta-OS is the initialization of recursion, regions, selectors,\nand hardware boundaries from an initial seed state into a sovereign\nrunning configuration.\n\n\\subsection{Seed State}\n\nDefine the seed:\n\n\n\\[\nX_0 = (\\mathbf{Reg}_0,\\; \\Sigma_0,\\; M_0,\\; B_0).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Boot Recursion}\n\nBoot is the finite unfolding:\n\n\n\\[\nX_{k+1} = \\mathcal{R}(X_k)\n\\quad\\text{for}\\quad\nk = 0,\\dots,n.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Sovereign Startup}\n\nStartup is complete when:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}(X_n) = X_n.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Region Spawn}\n\nRegions are spawned from a generator:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathbf{Reg}_0 = \\mathrm{Gen}_{boot}(\\Theta,\\hat{\\rho},\\Phi).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Selector Initialization}\n\nSelectors are initialized as:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_0 = \\Sigma_{kernel} \\cup \\Sigma_{UI} \\cup \\Sigma_{hw}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Boundary Activation}\n\nHardware and UI boundaries:\n\n\n\\[\nB_0(r) = \n\\begin{cases}\n1 & r \\in \\mathbf{Reg}_{hw} \\cup \\mathbf{Reg}_{UI} \\\\\n0 & \\text{otherwise}\n\\end{cases}\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Boot Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{Boot is the finite path from seed to sovereign recursion.}\n}\n\\section{Unified Deployment and Multi-Device Federation}\n\nDeployment in the Meta-OS is the instantiation of sovereign recursion\nacross multiple devices, regions, and networks, forming a federated\nunified state.\n\n\\subsection{Device Set}\n\nLet:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathbf{Dev} = \\{ d_1, d_2, \\dots, d_n \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\nEach device hosts a state:\n\n\n\\[\nX^{(d_i)}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Federated State}\n\nThe federated state is:\n\n\n\\[\nX^{fed} = \\bigcup_{i=1}^n X^{(d_i)}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Federated Recursion}\n\nFederated update:\n\n\n\\[\nX^{fed}_{t+1} = \\mathcal{R}(X^{fed}_t).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Deployment Map}\n\nDeployment is:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Delta : X \\to \\{ X^{(d_i)} \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Sovereign Federation}\n\nFederation is sovereign if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}(X^{fed}) = X^{fed}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Deployment Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{Deployment is the distribution of recursion across devices.}\n}\n\\section{Final Axioms and Closure of the Unified Meta-OS}\n\nThe Meta-OS is defined as a sovereign mathematical universe composed of\nregions, boundaries, selectors, fields, behaviors, recursion, and time.\nThe following axioms complete the system and establish closure.\n\n\\subsection{Axiom 1: Region Universality}\n\nAll computable entities are regions:\n\n\n\\[\n\\forall x \\in \\mathbf{Unified},\\; \\exists r \\in \\mathbf{Reg} : x \\subseteq r.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Axiom 2: Boundary Determinacy}\n\nBoundaries determine interaction:\n\n\n\\[\nB(r) = 0 \\Rightarrow r \\text{ is isolated}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Axiom 3: Selector Sovereignty}\n\nSelectors determine activation:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_k(r) = 1 \\Rightarrow r \\text{ participates in level } k.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Axiom 4: Recursion Closure}\n\nThe recursion operator is closed:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}(X) \\in \\mathbf{Unified}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Axiom 5: Sovereign State}\n\nA state is sovereign if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}(X) = X.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Axiom 6: Time Consistency}\n\nLogical time is monotonic:\n\n\n\\[\nT_{logic}(t+1) \\ge T_{logic}(t).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Axiom 7: Error Non-Sovereignty}\n\nErrors are non-sovereign states:\n\n\n\\[\nE(X) \\Leftrightarrow \\mathcal{R}(X) \\neq X.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Axiom 8: Recovery Existence}\n\nFor every non-sovereign state, a sovereign recovery exists:\n\n\n\\[\n\\forall X,\\; \\exists X' : \\mathcal{R}(X') = X'.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Axiom 9: Hardware Realizability}\n\nEvery recursion step corresponds to a realizable hardware step:\n\n\n\\[\n\\exists \\beta_{hw} : \\beta_{hw} \\circ \\mathcal{R} = \\mathcal{R}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Axiom 10: Federation Closure}\n\nFederated recursion is closed:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}\\left(\\bigcup_i X^{(d_i)}\\right)\n=\n\\bigcup_i \\mathcal{R}(X^{(d_i)}).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Closure Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{The Meta-OS is a closed, sovereign, recursion-complete mathematical universe.}\n}\\section{Unified Closure, Inference, and Meta-Recursion}\n\nMeta-recursion is the ability of the Meta-OS to generate new regions,\nselectors, boundaries, and behaviors from existing sovereign structures.\nInference is recursion applied to recursion.\n\n\\subsection{Meta-Recursion Operator}\n\nDefine the meta-recursion operator:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}^\\star : \\mathbf{Unified} \\to \\mathbf{Unified}\n\\]\n\n\n\nsuch that:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}^\\star(\\mathcal{R}) = \\mathcal{R}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Self-Generation of Regions}\n\nA region may generate new regions:\n\n\n\\[\nr' = \\Theta(r)\n\\quad\\text{with}\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(r') = r'.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Self-Generation of Selectors}\n\nSelectors may generate new selectors:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_{k+1} = \\Phi(\\Sigma_k)\n\\quad\\text{with}\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(\\Sigma_{k+1}) = \\Sigma_{k+1}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Self-Generation of Behaviors}\n\nBehaviors may generate new behaviors:\n\n\n\\[\n\\beta' = \\hat{\\rho}(\\beta)\n\\quad\\text{with}\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(\\beta') = \\beta'.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Inference}\n\nInference is:\n\n\n\\[\nX' = \\mathcal{R}(X)\n\\quad\\Rightarrow\\quad\nX'' = \\mathcal{R}(X').\n\\]\n\n\n\nThus inference is recursion applied to recursion.\n\n\\subsection{Unified Meta-Closure}\n\nThe system is meta-closed if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}^\\star(X) = X.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Meta-Recursion Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{Meta-recursion is recursion that generates recursion.}\n}\n\\section{Unified Finalization, Stability, and Sovereign Completion}\n\nFinalization is the stabilization of recursion, selectors, regions, and\nboundaries into a complete sovereign configuration. Stability is the\nabsence of non-sovereign transitions.\n\n\\subsection{Stability}\n\nA system is stable if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}(X) = X.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Completion}\n\nCompletion is the existence of a final sovereign state:\n\n\n\\[\n\\exists X^\\star : \\mathcal{R}(X^\\star) = X^\\star.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Selector Completion}\n\nSelectors are complete when:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_{k+1} = \\Sigma_k.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Region Completion}\n\nRegions are complete when:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Theta(r) = r.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Boundary Completion}\n\nBoundaries are complete when:\n\n\n\\[\nB(r) \\in \\{0,1\\} \\text{ is invariant under } \\mathcal{R}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Completion Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{Completion is the attainment of sovereign fixed-point stability.}\n}\n\n\\section{Unified Global Document Assembly}\n\nThe Meta-OS is the union of all sovereign components defined in Parts I–XXVIII.\nThe global system is constructed by assembling regions, selectors, boundaries,\nbehaviors, recursion, time, hardware, networking, storage, security, processes,\nvirtualization, federation, and meta-recursion into a single unified structure.\n\n\\subsection{Global State}\n\nThe global state is:\n\n\n\\[\nX_{global} = \\bigcup_{i=1}^{28} X^{(i)}\n\\]\n\n\nwhere each \\(X^{(i)}\\) is the sovereign state defined in Part \\(i\\).\n\n\\subsection{Global Recursion}\n\nGlobal recursion is:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}_{global}(X_{global}) = X_{global}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Global Selector Tower}\n\nThe selector tower is:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_{global} = \\bigcup_{k=0}^{\\omega} \\Sigma_k.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Global Region Universe}\n\nThe region universe is:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathbf{Reg}_{global} = \\bigcup_{i=1}^{28} \\mathbf{Reg}^{(i)}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Global Boundary Set}\n\nBoundaries:\n\n\n\\[\nB_{global} = \\bigcup_{i=1}^{28} B^{(i)}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Global Sovereignty}\n\nThe system is globally sovereign if:\n\n\n\\[\n\\mathcal{R}_{global}(X_{global}) = X_{global}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Assembly Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{The Meta-OS is the sovereign union of all recursion-complete components.}\n}\n\\section{Unified Final Manuscript Structure}\n\nThe complete Meta-OS specification is assembled as a sovereign manuscript\ncomposed of all recursion-complete sections. The manuscript is itself a\nregion with fields, boundaries, and behaviors.\n\n\\subsection{Document Region}\n\nDefine the document as a region:\n\n\n\\[\nr_{doc} \\in \\mathbf{Reg}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Document Fields}\n\nThe document fields are:\n\n\n\\[\nM(r_{doc}) = \\{ \\text{Parts I--XXIX},\\; \\text{references},\\; \\text{definitions} \\}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Document Boundary}\n\nThe document boundary is:\n\n\n\\[\nB(r_{doc}) = 1\n\\quad\\text{iff}\\quad\n\\text{the manuscript is externally accessible}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Document Behavior}\n\nDocument behavior is:\n\n\n\\[\n\\beta_{doc}(M(r_{doc})) = \\text{compile}(M(r_{doc})).\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Manuscript Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{The manuscript is a sovereign region containing the entire Meta-OS.}\n}\n\\section{Unified External Systems Integration and Generative Extensions}\n\nExternal systems, including artificial life, fractal organisms, generative\nmodels, and autonomous processes, are integrated into the Meta-OS as\nsovereign regions with their own behaviors, boundaries, and selectors.\n\n\\subsection{External Region Definition}\n\nAn external system is represented as:\n\n\n\\[\nr_{ext} \\in \\mathbf{Reg}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{External Behavior}\n\nIts behavior is:\n\n\n\\[\n\\beta_{ext} : M(r_{ext}) \\to M(r_{ext})'.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{External Boundary}\n\nExternal boundaries define interaction:\n\n\n\\[\nB(r_{ext}) : \\partial\\Omega_{ext} \\to [0,1].\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Selector Integration}\n\nExternal systems participate via:\n\n\n\\[\n\\Sigma_{ext}(r_{ext}) = 1.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Generative Extension}\n\nA generative extension is:\n\n\n\\[\nr_{gen} = \\Theta(r_{ext})\n\\quad\\text{with}\\quad\n\\mathcal{R}(r_{gen}) = r_{gen}.\n\\]\n\n\n\n\\subsection{Unified Integration Principle}\n\n\n\n\\[\n\\boxed{\n\\text{External generative systems become sovereign regions within the Meta-OS.}\n}\n\\section{Unified Complete Manuscript}\n\nThis manuscript contains the complete sovereign specification of the\nUnified Meta-OS, including all recursion-complete components defined in\nParts I–XXXI. The document is mathematically closed, sovereign, and\nself-consistent."

import argparse as _km_ap

def show_kernel_math(selector):
    """Print KERNEL MATH parts. Selector: a part number, 'all', 'doc', or 'list'."""
    if selector == 'list':
        print('KERNEL MATH parts available:', len(KERNEL_MATH_ORDER))
        for i in KERNEL_MATH_ORDER:
            print(f'  part {i} ({len(KERNEL_MATH_PARTS[str(i)])} block(s))')
        return
    if selector in ('doc', 'all-doc'):
        print(KERNEL_MATH_DOC)
        return
    if selector == 'all':
        for i in KERNEL_MATH_ORDER:
            print(f'# KERNEL MATH - PART {i}')
            print('\n'.join(KERNEL_MATH_PARTS[str(i)]))
            print()
        return
    try:
        n = int(selector)
    except ValueError:
        print('Unknown selector:', selector)
        print('Use: --math <number> | --math all | --math doc | --math list')
        return
    key = str(n)
    if key not in KERNEL_MATH_PARTS:
        print(f'No KERNEL MATH part {n}. Available: {KERNEL_MATH_ORDER}')
        return
    for block in KERNEL_MATH_PARTS[key]:
        print(f'# KERNEL MATH - PART {n}')
        print(block)
        print()

# ================================================================================================
# PART 1: SIX PRIMITIVES (Core Data Structures)
# ================================================================================================

@dataclass
class Thing:
    """⚬ thing — anything that exists (like Unreal's AActor)"""
    id: str
    name: str
    kind: str  # entity, place, object, concept, app, simulator
    properties: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    tags: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    parent_id: Optional[str] = None
    child_ids: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    created: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isoformat())
    modified: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isoformat())

@dataclass
class Becoming:
    """⚬ becoming — any process of change (like Unreal's Tick)"""
    id: str
    name: str
    thing_id: str
    kind: str  # movement, transformation, growth, simulation, quest
    progress: float = 0.0
    duration: float = 1.0
    is_looping: bool = False
    is_active: bool = True
    created: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isoformat())

@dataclass
class Witness:
    """⚬ witness — any observer (like Unreal's APlayerController)"""
    id: str
    name: str
    thing_id: Optional[str] = None
    focus_thing_id: Optional[str] = None
    memories: List[Dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    created: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isoformat())

@dataclass
class Memory:
    """⚬ memory — any persistent record (like Unreal's USaveGame)"""
    id: str
    content: str
    memory_type: str  # event, fact, relationship, snapshot
    thing_id: Optional[str] = None
    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    tick: int = 0

@dataclass
class Truth:
    """⚬ truth — any law or invariant (like Unreal's AGameMode)"""
    id: str
    statement: str
    truth_type: str  # physical, logical, moral, aesthetic, sovereign
    is_active: bool = True
    created: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isoformat())

@dataclass
class Sovereign:
    """⚬ sovereign — any self-owning entity (like Unreal's ownership)"""
    id: str
    name: str
    thing_id: str
    owned_thing_ids: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    rights: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)  # create, read, update, delete, witness
    created: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isoformat())


# ================================================================================================
# PART 2: GENESIS WORLD (Container for Six Primitives)
# ================================================================================================

class GenesisWorld:
    """The world container — holds all six primitives with SQLite persistence"""
    
    def __init__(self, name: str = "Untitled", db_path: str = None):
        self.name = name
        self.db_path = db_path or f"{name}.db"
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        self.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        self._init_db()
        
        self.things: Dict[str, Thing] = {}
        self.becomings: Dict[str, Becoming] = {}
        self.witnesses: Dict[str, Witness] = {}
        self.memories: List[Memory] = []
        self.truths: List[Truth] = []
        self.sovereigns: Dict[str, Sovereign] = {}
        
        self.tick_count = 0
        self.kino_mode = False
        self._load_from_db()
    
    def _init_db(self):
        """Initialize SQLite tables"""
        self.conn.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS worlds (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name TEXT UNIQUE,
                tick INTEGER,
                kino_mode INTEGER,
                created TEXT,
                modified TEXT
            )
        """)
        self.conn.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS things (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                name TEXT,
                kind TEXT,
                properties TEXT,
                tags TEXT,
                parent_id TEXT,
                child_ids TEXT,
                created TEXT,
                modified TEXT
            )
        """)
        self.conn.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS becomings (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                name TEXT,
                thing_id TEXT,
                kind TEXT,
                progress REAL,
                duration REAL,
                is_looping INTEGER,
                is_active INTEGER,
                created TEXT
            )
        """)
        self.conn.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS witnesses (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                name TEXT,
                thing_id TEXT,
                focus_thing_id TEXT,
                memories TEXT,
                created TEXT
            )
        """)
        self.conn.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memories (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                content TEXT,
                memory_type TEXT,
                thing_id TEXT,
                timestamp REAL,
                tick INTEGER
            )
        """)
        self.conn.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS truths (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                statement TEXT,
                truth_type TEXT,
                is_active INTEGER,
                created TEXT
            )
        """)
        self.conn.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sovereigns (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                name TEXT,
                thing_id TEXT,
                owned_thing_ids TEXT,
                rights TEXT,
                created TEXT
            )
        """)
        self.conn.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS plugins (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name TEXT UNIQUE,
                enabled INTEGER,
                config TEXT
            )
        """)
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def _load_from_db(self):
        """Load world state from database"""
        cur = self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM worlds WHERE name = ?", (self.name,))
        row = cur.fetchone()
        if row:
            self.tick_count = row["tick"]
            self.kino_mode = bool(row["kino_mode"])
        
        # Load things
        cur = self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM things")
        for row in cur:
            thing = Thing(
                id=row["id"],
                name=row["name"],
                kind=row["kind"],
                properties=json.loads(row["properties"]) if row["properties"] else {},
                tags=json.loads(row["tags"]) if row["tags"] else [],
                parent_id=row["parent_id"],
                child_ids=json.loads(row["child_ids"]) if row["child_ids"] else [],
                created=row["created"],
                modified=row["modified"]
            )
            self.things[thing.id] = thing
        
        # Load becomings
        cur = self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM becomings")
        for row in cur:
            becoming = Becoming(
                id=row["id"],
                name=row["name"],
                thing_id=row["thing_id"],
                kind=row["kind"],
                progress=row["progress"],
                duration=row["duration"],
                is_looping=bool(row["is_looping"]),
                is_active=bool(row["is_active"]),
                created=row["created"]
            )
            self.becomings[becoming.id] = becoming
        
        # Load witnesses
        cur = self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM witnesses")
        for row in cur:
            witness = Witness(
                id=row["id"],
                name=row["name"],
                thing_id=row["thing_id"],
                focus_thing_id=row["focus_thing_id"],
                memories=json.loads(row["memories"]) if row["memories"] else [],
                created=row["created"]
            )
            self.witnesses[witness.id] = witness
        
        # Load memories
        cur = self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM memories ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT 100")
        for row in cur:
            memory = Memory(
                id=row["id"],
                content=row["content"],
                memory_type=row["memory_type"],
                thing_id=row["thing_id"],
                timestamp=row["timestamp"],
                tick=row["tick"]
            )
            self.memories.append(memory)
        
        # Load truths
        cur = self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM truths WHERE is_active = 1")
        for row in cur:
            truth = Truth(
                id=row["id"],
                statement=row["statement"],
                truth_type=row["truth_type"],
                is_active=bool(row["is_active"]),
                created=row["created"]
            )
            self.truths.append(truth)
        
        # Load sovereigns
        cur = self.conn.execute("SELECT * FROM sovereigns")
        for row in cur:
            sovereign = Sovereign(
                id=row["id"],
                name=row["name"],
                thing_id=row["thing_id"],
                owned_thing_ids=json.loads(row["owned_thing_ids"]) if row["owned_thing_ids"] else [],
                rights=json.loads(row["rights"]) if row["rights"] else [],
                created=row["created"]
            )
            self.sovereigns[sovereign.id] = sovereign
    
    def save(self):
        """Save world state to database"""
        # Save world metadata
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO worlds (name, tick, kino_mode, created, modified)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, COALESCE((SELECT created FROM worlds WHERE name = ?), ?), ?)
        """, (self.name, self.tick_count, int(self.kino_mode), self.name, 
              datetime.now().isoformat(), datetime.now().isoformat()))
        
        # Save things
        for thing in self.things.values():
            self.conn.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO things (id, name, kind, properties, tags, parent_id, child_ids, created, modified)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (thing.id, thing.name, thing.kind, json.dumps(thing.properties), 
                  json.dumps(thing.tags), thing.parent_id, json.dumps(thing.child_ids),
                  thing.created, thing.modified))
        
        # Save becomings
        for becoming in self.becomings.values():
            self.conn.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO becomings (id, name, thing_id, kind, progress, duration, is_looping, is_active, created)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (becoming.id, becoming.name, becoming.thing_id, becoming.kind,
                  becoming.progress, becoming.duration, int(becoming.is_looping),
                  int(becoming.is_active), becoming.created))
        
        # Save witnesses
        for witness in self.witnesses.values():
            self.conn.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO witnesses (id, name, thing_id, focus_thing_id, memories, created)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (witness.id, witness.name, witness.thing_id, witness.focus_thing_id,
                  json.dumps(witness.memories[-100:]), witness.created))
        
        # Save memories (keep last 1000)
        for memory in self.memories[-1000:]:
            self.conn.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO memories (id, content, memory_type, thing_id, timestamp, tick)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (memory.id, memory.content, memory.memory_type, memory.thing_id,
                  memory.timestamp, memory.tick))
        
        # Save truths
        for truth in self.truths:
            self.conn.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO truths (id, statement, truth_type, is_active, created)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (truth.id, truth.statement, truth.truth_type, int(truth.is_active), truth.created))
        
        # Save sovereigns
        for sovereign in self.sovereigns.values():
            self.conn.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO sovereigns (id, name, thing_id, owned_thing_ids, rights, created)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (sovereign.id, sovereign.name, sovereign.thing_id,
                  json.dumps(sovereign.owned_thing_ids), json.dumps(sovereign.rights),
                  sovereign.created))
        
        self.conn.commit()
    
    # ===== Thing Management =====
    
    def create_thing(self, name: str, kind: str = "entity") -> Thing:
        thing = Thing(id=str(uuid4()), name=name, kind=kind)
        self.things[thing.id] = thing
        self.add_memory(f"✨ Created {name} [{kind}]", "event", thing.id)
        return thing
    
    def get_thing(self, thing_id: str) -> Optional[Thing]:
        return self.things.get(thing_id)
    
    def find_thing_by_name(self, name: str) -> Optional[Thing]:
        for thing in self.things.values():
            if thing.name.lower() == name.lower():
                return thing
        return None
    
    def find_things_by_kind(self, kind: str) -> List[Thing]:
        return [t for t in self.things.values() if t.kind == kind]
    
    def find_things_by_tag(self, tag: str) -> List[Thing]:
        return [t for t in self.things.values() if tag in t.tags]
    
    def set_property(self, thing_id: str, key: str, value: Any) -> bool:
        thing = self.things.get(thing_id)
        if thing:
            thing.properties[key] = value
            thing.modified = datetime.now().isoformat()
            self.add_memory(f"📖 {thing.name}.{key} = {value}", "fact", thing_id)
            return True
        return False
    
    def add_tag(self, thing_id: str, tag: str) -> bool:
        thing = self.things.get(thing_id)
        if thing and tag not in thing.tags:
            thing.tags.append(tag)
            return True
        return False
    
    # ===== Becoming Management =====
    
    def create_becoming(self, name: str, thing_id: str, kind: str, 
                        duration: float = 1.0, looping: bool = False) -> Becoming:
        becoming = Becoming(
            id=str(uuid4()),
            name=name,
            thing_id=thing_id,
            kind=kind,
            duration=duration,
            is_looping=looping
        )
        self.becomings[becoming.id] = becoming
        self.add_memory(f"🌀 Becoming '{name}' created for thing {thing_id}", "event", thing_id)
        return becoming
    
    def tick(self, delta: float = 1.0):
        """Advance all becomings"""
        if self.kino_mode:
            self.add_memory("⚠️ Cannot tick in Kino mode. Type 'intervene' first.", "warning")
            return
        
        self.tick_count += 1
        completed = []
        
        for bid, becoming in self.becomings.items():
            if becoming.is_active:
                becoming.progress += delta / becoming.duration
                if becoming.progress >= 1.0:
                    if becoming.is_looping:
                        becoming.progress = 0.0
                    else:
                        becoming.is_active = False
                        completed.append(bid)
                    self.add_memory(f"⚡ Becoming '{becoming.name}' at {becoming.progress:.0%}", 
                                   "progress", becoming.thing_id)
        
        for bid in completed:
            becoming = self.becomings[bid]
            self.add_memory(f"✅ Becoming '{becoming.name}' complete", "event", becoming.thing_id)
            del self.becomings[bid]
        
        self.add_memory(f"⚡ Tick {self.tick_count}", "tick")
    
    # ===== Witness Management =====
    
    def create_witness(self, name: str, thing_id: Optional[str] = None) -> Witness:
        witness = Witness(id=str(uuid4()), name=name, thing_id=thing_id)
        self.witnesses[witness.id] = witness
        self.add_memory(f"👁️ Witness '{name}' created", "event", thing_id)
        return witness
    
    def witness_observe(self, witness_id: str, focus_thing_id: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
        witness = self.witnesses.get(witness_id)
        if not witness:
            return f"❌ Witness '{witness_id}' not found"
        
        if focus_thing_id:
            witness.focus_thing_id = focus_thing_id
        
        thing = self.things.get(witness.focus_thing_id) if witness.focus_thing_id else None
        
        observation = {
            "tick": self.tick_count,
            "timestamp": time.time(),
            "focus": witness.focus_thing_id,
            "thing": thing.name if thing else None,
            "properties": thing.properties if thing else {},
            "kino_mode": self.kino_mode
        }
        witness.memories.append(observation)
        
        return f"📡 {witness.name} observes: {json.dumps(observation, indent=2)}"
    
    # ===== Memory Management =====
    
    def add_memory(self, content: str, memory_type: str = "event", thing_id: Optional[str] = None):
        memory = Memory(
            id=str(uuid4()),
            content=content,
            memory_type=memory_type,
            thing_id=thing_id,
            tick=self.tick_count
        )
        self.memories.append(memory)
        # Keep only last 1000 memories
        if len(self.memories) > 1000:
            self.memories = self.memories[-1000:]
    
    def get_memories(self, memory_type: Optional[str] = None, limit: int = 50) -> List[Memory]:
        if memory_type:
            return [m for m in self.memories if m.memory_type == memory_type][-limit:]
        return self.memories[-limit:]
    
    # ===== Truth Management =====
    
    def add_truth(self, statement: str, truth_type: str = "emergent") -> Truth:
        truth = Truth(id=str(uuid4()), statement=statement, truth_type=truth_type)
        self.truths.append(truth)
        self.add_memory(f"⚖️ Truth added: {statement}", "law")
        return truth
    
    def evaluate_truths(self) -> Dict[str, bool]:
        """Evaluate all active truths (placeholder - can be extended)"""
        return {t.statement: t.is_active for t in self.truths}
    
    # ===== Sovereign Management =====
    
    def create_sovereign(self, name: str, thing_id: str, rights: List[str] = None) -> Sovereign:
        sovereign = Sovereign(
            id=str(uuid4()),
            name=name,
            thing_id=thing_id,
            rights=rights or ["create", "read", "update", "delete", "witness"]
        )
        self.sovereigns[sovereign.id] = sovereign
        self.add_memory(f"👑 Sovereign '{name}' created for thing {thing_id}", "law", thing_id)
        return sovereign
    
    def transfer_ownership(self, thing_id: str, from_sovereign_id: str, to_sovereign_id: str) -> bool:
        from_sov = self.sovereigns.get(from_sovereign_id)
        to_sov = self.sovereigns.get(to_sovereign_id)
        if from_sov and to_sov and thing_id in from_sov.owned_thing_ids:
            from_sov.owned_thing_ids.remove(thing_id)
            to_sov.owned_thing_ids.append(thing_id)
            self.add_memory(f"📦 Ownership of {thing_id} transferred", "event", thing_id)
            return True
        return False
    
    # ===== Kino Mode (Observer Only) =====
    
    def enable_kino(self):
        self.kino_mode = True
        self.add_memory("🎒 Kino mode enabled: observer only", "mode")
    
    def disable_kino(self):
        self.kino_mode = False
        self.add_memory("🔫 Kino mode disabled: intervention allowed", "mode")
    
    # ===== World Management =====
    
    def fork(self, new_name: str) -> 'GenesisWorld':
        """Fork this world into a new parallel universe"""
        new_world = GenesisWorld(new_name)
        
        # Copy all things
        for thing in self.things.values():
            new_thing = Thing(
                id=str(uuid4()),
                name=thing.name,
                kind=thing.kind,
                properties=thing.properties.copy(),
                tags=thing.tags.copy(),
                parent_id=thing.parent_id,
                child_ids=thing.child_ids.copy()
            )
            new_world.things[new_thing.id] = new_thing
        
        # Copy truths
        for truth in self.truths:
            new_world.add_truth(truth.statement, truth.truth_type)
        
        new_world.add_memory(f"🌿 Forked from '{self.name}' at tick {self.tick_count}", "event")
        new_world.save()
        
        return new_world
    
    def snapshot(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Create a snapshot of current world state"""
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "tick": self.tick_count,
            "kino_mode": self.kino_mode,
            "things": {tid: {"name": t.name, "kind": t.kind, "properties": t.properties}
                      for tid, t in self.things.items()},
            "truths": [{"statement": t.statement} for t in self.truths],
            "memories": [{"content": m.content, "type": m.memory_type} for m in self.memories[-100:]]
        }
    
    def get_summary(self) -> str:
        """Get world summary as string"""
        return f"""
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║  WORLD: {self.name:<68}║
╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣
║  ⚬ things:      {len(self.things):3d}     ⚬ becomings:  {len(self.becomings):3d}     ⚬ witnesses:   {len(self.witnesses):3d}     ║
║  ⚬ memories:    {len(self.memories):3d}   ⚬ truths:     {len(self.truths):3d}     ⚬ sovereigns:  {len(self.sovereigns):3d}     ║
╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣
║  Tick: {self.tick_count:<6}     Kino mode: {'ON (observer only)' if self.kino_mode else 'OFF':<20}                     ║
║  Presence:     {min(100, len(self.witnesses) * 10):3d}%     Clarity:      {min(100, len(self.things) * 5):3d}%                     ║
║  Integration:  {min(100, len(self.truths) * 10):3d}%                                                                    ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
"""# ================================================================================================
# PART 3: PLUGIN SYSTEM (Geant4, GMAT, Blender, Windows, Android, VALARI)
# ================================================================================================

class Plugin:
    """Base class for all Genesis plugins"""
    
    def __init__(self, name: str, version: str, author: str):
        self.name = name
        self.version = version
        self.author = author
        self.world: Optional[GenesisWorld] = None
        self.enabled = False
    
    def register(self, world: GenesisWorld):
        self.world = world
        self.enabled = True
    
    def unregister(self):
        self.enabled = False
    
    def get_commands(self) -> Dict[str, Callable]:
        """Return dictionary of command names to handler functions"""
        return {}
    
    def get_becoming_types(self) -> Dict[str, Type]:
        """Return dictionary of becoming type names to classes"""
        return {}


class PluginManager:
    """Manages all loaded plugins"""
    
    def __init__(self, world: GenesisWorld):
        self.world = world
        self.plugins: Dict[str, Plugin] = {}
        self.commands: Dict[str, Tuple[Plugin, Callable]] = {}
        self.becoming_types: Dict[str, Type] = {}
    
    def register_plugin(self, plugin: Plugin):
        """Register a plugin with the manager"""
        plugin.register(self.world)
        self.plugins[plugin.name] = plugin
        
        # Register commands
        for cmd_name, handler in plugin.get_commands().items():
            self.commands[cmd_name] = (plugin, handler)
        
        # Register becoming types
        for type_name, becoming_class in plugin.get_becoming_types().items():
            self.becoming_types[type_name] = becoming_class
        
        self.world.add_memory(f"🔌 Plugin '{plugin.name}' v{plugin.version} loaded", "plugin")
        return f"✅ Plugin '{plugin.name}' loaded"
    
    def unregister_plugin(self, name: str):
        plugin = self.plugins.get(name)
        if plugin:
            plugin.unregister()
            del self.plugins[name]
            self.world.add_memory(f"🔌 Plugin '{name}' unloaded", "plugin")
            return f"✅ Plugin '{name}' unloaded"
        return f"❌ Plugin '{name}' not found"
    
    def execute_command(self, cmd_name: str, args: List[str]) -> str:
        if cmd_name in self.commands:
            plugin, handler = self.commands[cmd_name]
            return handler(args)
        return f"❌ Unknown plugin command: {cmd_name}"
    
    def create_becoming(self, type_name: str, name: str, thing_id: str, **kwargs) -> Optional[Becoming]:
        if type_name in self.becoming_types:
            becoming_class = self.becoming_types[type_name]
            return becoming_class(name=name, thing_id=thing_id, **kwargs)
        return None
    
    def list_plugins(self) -> List[str]:
        return list(self.plugins.keys())


# ===== GEANT4 PLUGIN (CERN Particle Simulation) =====

class Geant4Simulation(Becoming):
    """Geant4 particle simulation as a Genesis becoming"""
    
    def __init__(self, name: str, thing_id: str, macro_path: str, output_dir: str = None):
        super().__init__(
            id=str(uuid4()),
            name=name,
            thing_id=thing_id,
            kind="geant4",
            duration=0.0,  # Continuous until stopped
            is_looping=False
        )
        self.macro_path = macro_path
        self.output_dir = output_dir or tempfile.mkdtemp()
        self.process = None
        self.output = ""
    
    def start(self):
        """Start the Geant4 simulation"""
        cmd = ["geant4", self.macro_path]
        try:
            self.process = subprocess.Popen(
                cmd, 
                cwd=self.output_dir,
                stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
                text=True
            )
            return f"🚀 Geant4 simulation '{self.name}' started"
        except FileNotFoundError:
            return f"❌ Geant4 not found. Install with: sudo apt install geant4"
    
    def stop(self):
        """Stop the simulation"""
        if self.process:
            self.process.terminate()
            self.process = None
            return f"⏹️ Geant4 simulation '{self.name}' stopped"
        return f"⚠️ No running simulation"
    
    def get_output(self) -> str:
        """Get simulation output as witness"""
        hits_file = os.path.join(self.output_dir, "hits.csv")
        if os.path.exists(hits_file):
            with open(hits_file, 'r') as f:
                return f.read()[:5000]
        return "No output yet. Simulation may still be running."


class Geant4Plugin(Plugin):
    """Plugin for CERN Geant4 particle simulations"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(name="geant4", version="1.0.0", author="Darrell Lee Stiltner")
        self.active_simulations: Dict[str, Geant4Simulation] = {}
    
    def get_commands(self) -> Dict[str, Callable]:
        return {
            "geant4_run": self.cmd_run,
            "geant4_stop": self.cmd_stop,
            "geant4_output": self.cmd_output,
            "geant4_list": self.cmd_list
        }
    
    def get_becoming_types(self) -> Dict[str, Type]:
        return {"geant4": Geant4Simulation}
    
    def cmd_run(self, args: List[str]) -> str:
        """Usage: geant4_run <name> <macro_path>"""
        if len(args) < 2:
            return "Usage: geant4_run <name> <macro_path>"
        name = args[0]
        macro_path = args[1]
        
        sim = Geant4Simulation(name, "plugin_thing", macro_path)
        self.active_simulations[name] = sim
        return sim.start()
    
    def cmd_stop(self, args: List[str]) -> str:
        if not args:
            return "Usage: geant4_stop <name>"
        sim = self.active_simulations.get(args[0])
        if sim:
            return sim.stop()
        return f"❌ Simulation '{args[0]}' not found"
    
    def cmd_output(self, args: List[str]) -> str:
        if not args:
            return "Usage: geant4_output <name>"
        sim = self.active_simulations.get(args[0])
        if sim:
            return sim.get_output()
        return f"❌ Simulation '{args[0]}' not found"
    
    def cmd_list(self, args: List[str]) -> str:
        if not self.active_simulations:
            return "No active Geant4 simulations"
        return "Active simulations:\n  " + "\n  ".join(self.active_simulations.keys())


# ===== GMAT PLUGIN (NASA Orbital Mechanics) =====

class GMATSimulation(Becoming):
    """NASA GMAT orbital mechanics simulation"""
    
    def __init__(self, name: str, thing_id: str, script_path: str):
        super().__init__(
            id=str(uuid4()),
            name=name,
            thing_id=thing_id,
            kind="gmat",
            duration=0.0,
            is_looping=False
        )
        self.script_path = script_path
        self.process = None
        self.output = ""
    
    def run(self) -> str:
        cmd = ["GMAT", self.script_path]
        try:
            self.process = subprocess.Popen(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True)
            stdout, stderr = self.process.communicate(timeout=30)
            self.output = stdout
            return f"🛰️ GMAT simulation '{self.name}' completed\n{stdout[:1000]}"
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            self.process.kill()
            return f"⚠️ GMAT simulation '{self.name}' timed out"
        except FileNotFoundError:
            return f"❌ GMAT not found. Install from: https://gmat.gsfc.nasa.gov/"


class GMATPlugin(Plugin):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(name="gmat", version="1.0.0", author="Darrell Lee Stiltner")
        self.active_simulations: Dict[str, GMATSimulation] = {}
    
    def get_commands(self) -> Dict[str, Callable]:
        return {
            "gmat_run": self.cmd_run,
            "gmat_status": self.cmd_status
        }
    
    def get_becoming_types(self) -> Dict[str, Type]:
        return {"gmat": GMATSimulation}
